 143:     /** Read-only discovery of absent expected identities in the active quality window. */
 144:     private List<Candidate> gapCandidates(Instant exchangeNow,Instant databaseNow){
 145:         List<Candidate> result=new ArrayList<>();
 146:         for(String symbol:coins.enabledSymbols()) for(String interval:properties.getIntervals()) {
 147:             long seconds=intervalSeconds(interval), window=Math.max(1,minimumCandles)*seconds;
 148:             Instant eligible=exchangeNow.minusSeconds(liveCloseGraceSeconds+safetyMarginSeconds);
 149:             Instant first=Instant.ofEpochSecond((exchangeNow.minusSeconds(window).getEpochSecond()/seconds)*seconds);
 150:             Set<Instant> present=new HashSet<>(jdbc.query("""
 151:                 SELECT open_time FROM candle WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND open_time>=? AND open_time<?
 152:                 """,(rs,n)->rs.getTimestamp(1).toInstant(),symbol,interval,Timestamp.from(first),Timestamp.from(eligible)));
 153:             for(Instant open=first;open.plusSeconds(seconds).compareTo(eligible)<=0;open=open.plusSeconds(seconds)) {
 154:                 if(!present.contains(open) && audit.due(symbol,interval,open,databaseNow))
 155:                     result.add(new Candidate(symbol,interval,open,open.plusSeconds(seconds).minusMillis(1)));
 156:             }
 157:         }
 158:         return result;
 159:     }
 160: 
 161:     private void recover(Candidate candidate,Instant exchangeNow,Instant databaseNow,boolean gap) {
 162:         var prior=audit.findCandle(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime);
 163:         int attempts=prior.map(s->s.attempts()+1).orElse(1);
 164:         try {
 165:             List<BinanceKline> rows=binance.getKlines(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime,candidate.closeTime);
 166:             List<BinanceKline> exact=rows.stream().filter(k->k.openTime().equals(candidate.openTime)&&k.closeTime().equals(candidate.closeTime)).toList();
 167:             if(rows.isEmpty() && gap) {
 168:                 List<BinanceKline> first=binance.getKlinesAtOrAfter(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime);
 169:                 if(!first.isEmpty() && first.getFirst().openTime().isAfter(candidate.openTime)) {
 170:                     terminalUnavailable(candidate,exchangeNow,"NOT_ON_EXCHANGE");return;
 171:                 }
 172:                 pending(candidate,attempts,databaseNow,"REST_EMPTY",exchangeNow);return;
 173:             }
 174:             if(rows.isEmpty()) { pending(candidate,attempts,databaseNow,"REST_EMPTY",exchangeNow);return; }
 175:             if(candidate.closeTime.compareTo(exchangeNow)>=0) {
 176:                 pending(candidate,attempts,databaseNow,"REST_NOT_FINAL",exchangeNow);return;
 177:             }
 178:             if(exact.size()!=1) {
 179:                 pending(candidate,attempts,databaseNow,"REST_MISMATCH",exchangeNow);return;
 180:             }
 181:             BinanceKline k=exact.getFirst();
 182:             tx.executeWithoutResult(status->{
 183:                 boolean changed=gap ? candles.insertAuthoritativeGapBackfill(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime,k.closeTime(),
 184:                     k.openPrice(),k.highPrice(),k.lowPrice(),k.closePrice(),k.volume(),k.quoteAssetVolume(),k.numberOfTrades(),k.takerBuyBaseVolume(),k.takerBuyQuoteVolume())
 185:                     : candles.finalizeExistingOpenFromAuthoritative(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime,k.closeTime(),
 186:                     k.openPrice(),k.highPrice(),k.lowPrice(),k.closePrice(),k.volume(),k.quoteAssetVolume(),k.numberOfTrades(),k.takerBuyBaseVolume(),k.takerBuyQuoteVolume());
 187:                 String result=changed ? (gap?"GAP_BACKFILLED":"RECOVERED") : (gap?"ALREADY_PRESENT":"ALREADY_CLOSED");
 188:                 audit.terminal(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime,result,null,exchangeNow,k.openTime(),k.closeTime());
 189:                 if(changed) outcomes.record(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime,"SKIPPED_NOT_LIVE",result,null,gap?"AUTHORITATIVE_GAP_BACKFILL":"AUTHORITATIVE_RECOVERY");
 190:             });
 191:         } catch(RuntimeException failure) { pending(candidate,attempts,databaseNow,"REST_FAILURE",exchangeNow); }
 192:     }
 193: 
 194:     private void pending(Candidate c,int attempts,Instant databaseNow,String reason,Instant exchangeNow){
 195:         long delay=Math.min(300,attempts==1?5:attempts==2?15:60L*(1L<<Math.min(2,attempts-3)));
 196:         audit.pending(c.symbol,c.interval,c.openTime,attempts,databaseNow.plusSeconds(delay),reason,exchangeNow);
 197:     }
 198:     private boolean positiveLossEvidence(Candidate candidate){
 199:         // This relies on the current FIX-131 invariant: one primary WebSocket
 200:         // session invokes processKline synchronously and in Binance receive
 201:         // order. If streams are sharded or processing becomes asynchronous,
 202:         // this successor proof must be redesigned and re-tested.
 203:         Instant successor=candidate.openTime.plusSeconds(intervalSeconds(candidate.interval));
 204:         Integer successorPresent=jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM candle WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND open_time=?",Integer.class,
 205:             candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,Timestamp.from(successor));
 206:         return successorPresent!=null&&successorPresent>0 || (lastReconnectAdmission!=null && !lastReconnectAdmission.isBefore(candidate.closeTime));
 207:     }
 208:     private void terminalUnavailable(Candidate candidate,Instant exchangeNow,String reason){
 209:         tx.executeWithoutResult(status->{
 210:             audit.terminal(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime,"EXPIRED",reason,exchangeNow,null,null);
 211:             outcomes.record(candidate.symbol,candidate.interval,candidate.openTime,"SKIPPED_DATA_UNAVAILABLE",reason,null,"AUTHORITATIVE_RECONCILIATION");
 212:         });
 213:     }
 214:     private static long intervalSeconds(String interval){return switch(interval){case "1m"->60;case "5m"->300;case "15m"->900;case "1h"->3600;case "4h"->14400;case "1d"->86400;default->throw new IllegalArgumentException("Unsupported interval: "+interval);};}
 215:     private static Instant expectedClose(Instant open,String interval){return open.plusSeconds(intervalSeconds(interval)).minusMillis(1);}
 216:     private record Candidate(String symbol,String interval,Instant openTime,Instant closeTime) {}
 217: }

DETECTOR
   1: package com.crypto.local;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.administration.service.CoinConfigurationService;
   4: import com.crypto.indicator.event.*;
   5: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
   6: import org.springframework.scheduling.annotation.Scheduled;
   7: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value;
   8: import org.springframework.jdbc.core.JdbcTemplate;
   9: import org.slf4j.*;
  10: import java.time.*;
  11: import java.sql.Timestamp;
  12: import java.util.*;
  13: 
  14: /** FIX-153: local durable candles close the commit-to-memory-dispatch crash window.
  15:  * BACKFILL is never admitted. Legacy NULL provenance is eligible only when the
  16:  * live stream wrote a close-transition token.
  17:  * Small lookback and one admission per idle lane keep detector below live work. */
  18: @Component
  19: public class LocalClosedCandleDetector {
  20:     private static final Logger log=LoggerFactory.getLogger(LocalClosedCandleDetector.class);
  21:     private final JdbcTemplate jdbc;private final CoinConfigurationService coins;
  22:     private final CandleAnalysisDispatcher dispatcher;private final CandleAnalysisExecutionCoordinator coordination;
  23:     private final com.crypto.client.config.binance.BinanceMarketDataProperties properties;
  24:     @Value("${shared-market.mode:OFF}") private String mode;
  25:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired(required=false) private ExchangeClockSynchronizer exchangeClock;
  26:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired(required=false) private CandleAnalysisOutcomeStore outcomes;
  27:     private int nextLane;
  28:     private final Map<String,Retry> retries=new HashMap<>();
  29:     private final Map<String,Instant> analysedUntil=new HashMap<>();
  30:     public LocalClosedCandleDetector(JdbcTemplate jdbc,CoinConfigurationService coins,CandleAnalysisDispatcher dispatcher,CandleAnalysisExecutionCoordinator coordination,com.crypto.client.config.binance.BinanceMarketDataProperties properties){
  31:         this.jdbc=new JdbcTemplate(Objects.requireNonNull(jdbc.getDataSource()));this.jdbc.setQueryTimeout(2);
  32:         this.coins=coins;this.dispatcher=dispatcher;this.coordination=coordination;this.properties=properties;
  33:     }
  34:     @Scheduled(initialDelay=5000,fixedDelayString="${local-candle.detector-delay-ms:2000}")
  35:     public void detect(){
  36:         if(!"OFF".equals(mode))return;
  37:         Instant localNow=Instant.now();Instant now=exchangeClock==null?localNow:exchangeClock.correctJvm(localNow);long deadline=System.nanoTime()+java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit.SECONDS.toNanos(2);
  38:         try {
  39:             var symbols=coins.enabledSymbols();var intervals=properties.getIntervals();
  40:             int total=symbols.size()*intervals.size();
  41:             for(int checked=0;checked<total;checked++){
  42:                 if(System.nanoTime()>deadline)return;
  43:                 // FIX-153: rotate across bounded passes so a slow first lane cannot starve later symbols.
  44:                 int lane=Math.floorMod(nextLane++,total);
  45:                 String symbol=symbols.get(lane/intervals.size()),interval=intervals.get(lane%intervals.size());
  46:                 if(!dispatcher.idle(symbol,interval))continue;
  47:                 long lookback=Math.max(180,com.crypto.shared.SharedEventPolicy.closeGraceSeconds(interval)*2L);
  48:                 // Index is on open_time: include one full interval before the close-age lookback.
  49:                 Instant lowerOpen=now.minusSeconds(intervalSeconds(interval)+lookback);
  50:                 var candidates=jdbc.query("SELECT open_time,close_time FROM candle WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND closed=1 AND close_transition_token IS NOT NULL AND (ingestion_source IS NULL OR ingestion_source<>'BACKFILL') AND open_time>=? AND close_time<=? ORDER BY open_time ASC LIMIT 4",
  51:                     (rs,n)->new CandleClosedEvent(symbol,interval,rs.getTimestamp(1).toInstant(),rs.getTimestamp(2).toInstant()),symbol,interval,Timestamp.from(lowerOpen),Timestamp.from(now));
  52:                 for(var event:candidates){
  53:                     String retryKey=key(event);
  54:                     Instant knownAnalysedUntil=analysedUntil.get(retryKey);
  55:                     if(knownAnalysedUntil!=null && knownAnalysedUntil.isAfter(now))continue;
  56:                     Retry retry=retries.get(retryKey);
  57:                     if(retry!=null && retry.nextAttempt().isAfter(now))continue;
  58:                     // A healthy analysis is terminal for detector purposes. Do this
  59:                     // before dispatcher admission so ordinary completed candles never
  60:                     // become retry/expiry noise.
  61:                     if(!missing(event)){retries.remove(retryKey);analysedUntil.put(retryKey,eligibilityEndsAfter(event,lookback));continue;}
  62:                     // No database fence; existing striped candle lock covers only check+handoff.
  63:                     var acquired=coordination.tryLock(symbol,interval,event.openTime());
  64:                     if(acquired.isEmpty())continue;
  65:                     try(var ignored=acquired.get()){
  66:                         if(dispatcher.submitDetected(event,()->missing(event))){
  67:                             // Admission is not completion. Retain bounded state until
  68:                             // durable signal/work evidence appears on a later pass.
  69:                             // Every re-admission consumes the same bounded retry budget;
  70:                             // a repeatedly failing worker must not execute forever.
  71:                             recordAdmission(retryKey,event,now,lookback);
  72:                             break;
  73:                         }
  74:                         defer(retryKey,event,now,"NOT_ADMITTED");
  75:                     }
  76:                 }
  77:             }
  78:             for(var iterator=retries.entrySet().iterator();iterator.hasNext();){
  79:                 var entry=iterator.next();
  80:                 // Retention is strictly after the SQL lower-open-time boundary.
  81:                 // Therefore pruning cannot make a still-detectable candle restart
  82:                 // with a fresh retry budget.
  83:                 if(entry.getValue().retainUntil().isBefore(now)){
  84:                     log.info("[FIX-153][DETECTOR_RETRY_PRUNED] candle={}, attempts={}, reason=NO_LONGER_ELIGIBLE",entry.getKey(),entry.getValue().attempts());
  85:                     iterator.remove();
  86:                 }
  87:             }
  88:             analysedUntil.entrySet().removeIf(e->!e.getValue().isAfter(now));
  89:         }catch(RuntimeException failure){LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.Event.DETECTOR_FAILED);
  90:             log.warn("[FIX-153][DETECTOR_FAILED] no checkpoint advanced; next bounded pass may retry",failure);}
  91:     }
  92:     private static String key(CandleClosedEvent e){return e.symbol()+"|"+e.intervalCode()+"|"+e.openTime();}
  93:     private void defer(String key,CandleClosedEvent event,Instant now,String reason){
  94:         Retry prior=retries.get(key);int attempts=prior==null?1:prior.attempts()+1;
  95:         long delay=Math.min(60,1L<<Math.min(6,attempts));
  96:         long lookback=Math.max(180,com.crypto.shared.SharedEventPolicy.closeGraceSeconds(event.intervalCode())*2L);
  97:         Instant retryDeadline=event.closeTime().plusSeconds(lookback);
  98:         Instant retainUntil=eligibilityEndsAfter(event,lookback);
  99:         if(attempts>6 || !retryDeadline.isAfter(now)){
 100:             // Keep a terminal marker until this candle has aged out of the SQL
 101:             // lookback. Removing it immediately would restart the retry budget.
 102:             retries.put(key,new Retry(attempts,Instant.MAX,retryDeadline,retainUntil));
 103:             recordExpired(event,reason);
 104:             log.warn("[FIX-153][DETECTOR_GAP_EXPIRED] candle={}, attempts={}, reason={}",key,attempts,reason);return;
 105:         }
 106:         retries.put(key,new Retry(attempts,now.plusSeconds(delay),retryDeadline,retainUntil));
 107:         log.info("[FIX-153][DETECTOR_RETRY_DEFERRED] candle={}, attempts={}, nextUtc={}, reason={}",key,attempts,now.plusSeconds(delay),reason);
 108:     }
 109:     private void recordAdmission(String key,CandleClosedEvent event,Instant now,long lookback){
 110:         Retry prior=retries.get(key);int attempts=prior==null?1:prior.attempts()+1;
 111:         Instant retryDeadline=event.closeTime().plusSeconds(lookback);
 112:         Instant retainUntil=eligibilityEndsAfter(event,lookback);
 113:         if(attempts>6 || !retryDeadline.isAfter(now)){
 114:             // See defer(): this marker is deliberately retained until the query
 115:             // can no longer rediscover this candle.
 116:             retries.put(key,new Retry(attempts,Instant.MAX,retryDeadline,retainUntil));
 117:             recordExpired(event,"ADMISSION_RETRY_LIMIT");
 118:             log.warn("[FIX-153][DETECTOR_GAP_EXPIRED] candle={}, attempts={}, reason=ADMISSION_RETRY_LIMIT",key,attempts);
 119:             return;
 120:         }
 121:         long delay=retryDelaySeconds(attempts);
 122:         retries.put(key,new Retry(attempts,now.plusSeconds(delay),retryDeadline,retainUntil));
 123:         log.info("[FIX-153][DETECTOR_ADMITTED_AWAITING_EVIDENCE] candle={}, attempts={}, nextUtc={}",key,attempts,now.plusSeconds(delay));
 124:     }
 125:     /** Strictly after the candidate predicate: open_time >= now - interval - lookback. */
 126:     private static Instant eligibilityEndsAfter(CandleClosedEvent event,long lookback){
 127:         return event.openTime().plusSeconds(intervalSeconds(event.intervalCode())+lookback+1);
 128:     }
 129:     private static long retryDelaySeconds(int attempts){return Math.min(60,1L<<Math.min(6,attempts));}
 130:     private record Retry(int attempts,Instant nextAttempt,Instant retryDeadline,Instant retainUntil) {}
 131:     private static long intervalSeconds(String interval){
 132:         return switch(interval){case "1m"->60;case "5m"->300;case "15m"->900;case "1h"->3600;case "4h"->14400;case "1d"->86400;
 133:             default->throw new IllegalArgumentException("FIX-153 unsupported detector interval: "+interval);};
 134:     }
 135:     private boolean missing(CandleClosedEvent e){
 136:         if(outcomes!=null && outcomes.exists(e.symbol(),e.intervalCode(),e.openTime()))return false;
 137:         return jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT (EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM trade_signal WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND candle_open_time=?) OR EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM signal_processing_work WHERE symbol=? AND interval_code=? AND candle_open_time=?))",Integer.class,
 138:             e.symbol(),e.intervalCode(),Timestamp.from(e.openTime()),e.symbol(),e.intervalCode(),Timestamp.from(e.openTime()))==0;
 139:     }
 140:     private void recordExpired(CandleClosedEvent event,String reason){
 141:         if(outcomes!=null)outcomes.record(event.symbol(),event.intervalCode(),event.openTime(),"ANALYSIS_EXPIRED",reason,null,"LOCAL_DETECTOR");
 142:     }
 143: }

DISPATCH
   1: package com.crypto.indicator.event;
   2: 
   3: import jakarta.annotation.PreDestroy;
   4: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Qualifier;
   5: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
   6: import org.slf4j.Logger;
   7: import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
   8: import com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics;
   9: import static com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.Event.*;
  10: import java.time.Instant;
  11: import java.util.*;
  12: import java.util.concurrent.*;
  13: 
  14: /** FIX-153: one running task per lane. 1m keeps latest pending; higher TFs retain
  15:  * a bounded FIFO. Reservations and admission share the lane monitor, including
  16:  * detector idle check. No analysis executes on ingestion threads at saturation. */
  17: @Component
  18: public class CandleAnalysisDispatcher {
  19:     private static final Logger log=LoggerFactory.getLogger(CandleAnalysisDispatcher.class);
  20:     private final Executor executor;
  21:     private final CandleClosedAnalysisWorker worker;
  22:     private final Map<String,Lane> lanes=new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
  23:     private volatile boolean stopped;
  24:     public CandleAnalysisDispatcher(@Qualifier("candleAnalysisExecutor") Executor executor,CandleClosedAnalysisWorker worker){this.executor=executor;this.worker=worker;}
  25:     public void submit(CandleClosedEvent event){admit(event,false,()->true);}
  26:     /** Check exact candle evidence under the detector's existing in-process lock.
  27:      * Check+reserve+enqueue is atomic with live admission; detector never replaces pending live work. */
  28:     public boolean submitDetected(CandleClosedEvent event,java.util.function.BooleanSupplier missing){return admit(event,true,missing);}
  29:     public boolean idle(String symbol,String interval){Lane l=lanes.get(symbol+"|"+interval);if(l==null)return !stopped;synchronized(l){return !stopped&&l.active==null&&l.pending.isEmpty();}}
  30:     /** A detector reservation is in-flight admission work, even before a task exists. */
  31:     public boolean idleAll(){return !stopped && lanes.values().stream().allMatch(l->{synchronized(l){return l.active==null&&l.pending.isEmpty()&&l.reserved.isEmpty()&&l.deferredLive.isEmpty();}});}
  32:     private boolean admit(CandleClosedEvent e,boolean detector,java.util.function.BooleanSupplier missing){
  33:         if(e==null || stopped)return false;
  34:         String key=e.symbol().trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT)+"|"+e.intervalCode();
  35:         Lane lane=lanes.computeIfAbsent(key,k->new Lane());
  36:         if(detector){
  37:             synchronized(lane){
  38:                 if(stopped || lane.active!=null || !lane.pending.isEmpty() || !lane.reserved.add(e.openTime())) return false;
  39:             }
  40:             // FIX-153: reserve first, but never hold admission's monitor across database I/O.
  41:             boolean absent;
  42:             try {absent=missing.getAsBoolean();}
  43:             catch(RuntimeException failure){
  44:                 CandleClosedEvent handoff;
  45:                 synchronized(lane){
  46:                     handoff=lane.deferredLive.remove(e.openTime());
  47:                     if(handoff!=null)enqueueReserved(lane,key,handoff,false);
  48:                     else lane.reserved.remove(e.openTime());
  49:                 }
  50:                 throw failure;
  51:             }
  52:             synchronized(lane){
  53:                 // A committed live close that arrived during the detector's database
  54:                 // check owns admission. Transfer it to the queue while retaining
  55:                 // its reservation and the lane monitor: no second SQL check and no
  56:                 // interleaving admission window can lose it.
  57:                 CandleClosedEvent liveHandoff=lane.deferredLive.remove(e.openTime());
  58:                 if(liveHandoff!=null)return enqueueReserved(lane,key,liveHandoff,false);
  59:                 if(!absent || stopped || lane.active!=null || !lane.pending.isEmpty()){
  60:                     lane.reserved.remove(e.openTime());return false;
  61:                 }
  62:             }
  63:         }
  64:         synchronized(lane){
  65:             if(stopped || (detector && (lane.active!=null || !lane.pending.isEmpty()))){if(detector)lane.reserved.remove(e.openTime());return false;}
  66:             if(!detector && lane.reserved.contains(e.openTime())){
  67:                 // Detector owns the temporary reservation only. Preserve the exact
  68:                 // committed event and let the detector hand it straight back once
  69:                 // its read completes; no live close is discarded in this window.
  70:                 lane.deferredLive.putIfAbsent(e.openTime(),e);return true;
  71:             }
  72:             if(!detector && !lane.reserved.add(e.openTime())){LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(DUPLICATE_ADMISSION);return false;}
  73:             Task task=new Task(e,detector);
  74:             if("1m".equals(e.intervalCode()) && !lane.pending.isEmpty()){
  75:                 Task previous=lane.pending.peekLast();
  76:                 // A delayed older callback cannot replace newer pending work.
  77:                 if(!e.openTime().isAfter(previous.event.openTime())){lane.reserved.remove(e.openTime());LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(OVERFLOW);return false;}
  78:                 previous=lane.pending.removeLast();lane.reserved.remove(previous.event.openTime());lane.deferredLive.remove(previous.event.openTime());LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(REPLACED);
  79:                 log.info("[FIX-153][PENDING_REPLACED] lane={}, skippedOpenUtc={}, keptOpenUtc={}, businessExecuted=false",key,previous.event.openTime(),e.openTime());
  80:             }else if(lane.pending.size()>=4){lane.reserved.remove(e.openTime());LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(OVERFLOW);
  81:                 log.info("[FIX-153][LANE_OVERFLOW] lane={}, rejectedOpenUtc={}, pending={}, policy=BOUNDED_FIFO",key,e.openTime(),lane.pending.size());return false;}
  82:             lane.pending.add(task);LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(ADMITTED);
  83:             if(detector)LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(DETECTOR_ADMITTED);
  84:             return schedule(lane,key);
  85:         }
  86:     }
  87:     /** Caller holds lane's monitor and has already retained e.openTime() in reserved. */
  88:     private boolean enqueueReserved(Lane lane,String key,CandleClosedEvent e,boolean detector){
  89:         if(stopped){lane.reserved.remove(e.openTime());return false;}
  90:         Task task=new Task(e,detector);
  91:         if("1m".equals(e.intervalCode()) && !lane.pending.isEmpty()){
  92:             Task previous=lane.pending.peekLast();
  93:             if(!e.openTime().isAfter(previous.event.openTime())){lane.reserved.remove(e.openTime());LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(OVERFLOW);return false;}
  94:             previous=lane.pending.removeLast();lane.reserved.remove(previous.event.openTime());lane.deferredLive.remove(previous.event.openTime());LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(REPLACED);
  95:             log.info("[FIX-153][PENDING_REPLACED] lane={}, skippedOpenUtc={}, keptOpenUtc={}, businessExecuted=false",key,previous.event.openTime(),e.openTime());
  96:         }else if(lane.pending.size()>=4){lane.reserved.remove(e.openTime());LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(OVERFLOW);
  97:             log.info("[FIX-153][LANE_OVERFLOW] lane={}, rejectedOpenUtc={}, pending={}, policy=BOUNDED_FIFO",key,e.openTime(),lane.pending.size());return false;}
  98:         lane.pending.add(task);LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(ADMITTED);
  99:         if(detector)LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(DETECTOR_ADMITTED);
 100:         return schedule(lane,key);
 101:     }
 102:     private boolean schedule(Lane lane,String key){
 103:         if(stopped)return false;
 104:         if(lane.active!=null)return true;
 105:         Task task=lane.pending.pollFirst();if(task==null)return true;lane.active=task;
 106:         try {executor.execute(()->{
 107:             synchronized(lane){if(stopped || lane.active!=task)return;}
 108:             long start=System.nanoTime();LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(ANALYSIS_ATTEMPTED);LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.analysis(key,Instant.now());
 109:             String outcome="THREW_OR_UNCONFIRMED";
 110:             try {outcome=worker.processShared(task.event,task.event.closeTime(),true);}
 111:             catch(RuntimeException failure){log.error("[FIX-153][ANALYSIS_TASK_FAILED] lane={}, openUtc={}",key,task.event.openTime(),failure);}
 112:             finally {
 113:                 LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(ANALYSIS_FINISHED);
 114:                 log.debug("[FIX-153][ANALYSIS_TASK_RETURNED] lane={}, openUtc={}, outcome={}, callbackMs={}; task return alone is not wallet commit evidence",key,task.event.openTime(),outcome,(System.nanoTime()-start)/1_000_000);
 115:                 synchronized(lane){lane.reserved.remove(task.event.openTime());lane.deferredLive.remove(task.event.openTime());lane.active=null;schedule(lane,key);}
 116:             }
 117:         });return true;}catch(RejectedExecutionException failure){
 118:             // Release every queued reservation on rejection: local detector may reconsider gaps.
 119:             LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(SUBMISSION_REJECTED);lane.active=null;lane.reserved.remove(task.event.openTime());
 120:             lane.deferredLive.remove(task.event.openTime());
 121:             for(Task queued:lane.pending){lane.reserved.remove(queued.event.openTime());lane.deferredLive.remove(queued.event.openTime());}lane.pending.clear();
 122:             log.info("[FIX-153][EXECUTOR_REJECTED] lane={}, openUtc={}, ranOnIngestionThread=false",key,task.event.openTime());
 123:             return false;
 124:         }
 125:     }
 126:     public Map<String,Object> snapshot(){Map<String,Object> result=new TreeMap<>();lanes.forEach((k,l)->{synchronized(l){result.put(k,Map.of("active",l.active!=null,"pending",l.pending.size(),"reservations",l.reserved.size()));}});return result;}
 127:     @PreDestroy public void stop(){stopped=true;lanes.values().forEach(l->{synchronized(l){l.pending.clear();l.reserved.clear();l.deferredLive.clear();}});}
 128:     private static class Lane {final Deque<Task> pending=new ArrayDeque<>();final Set<Instant> reserved=new HashSet<>();final Map<Instant,CandleClosedEvent> deferredLive=new HashMap<>();Task active;}
 129:     private record Task(CandleClosedEvent event,boolean detector) {}
 130: }

TX
   1: package com.crypto.infrastructure.transaction;
   2: 
   3: import org.slf4j.Logger;
   4: import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
   5: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
   6: import org.springframework.transaction.PlatformTransactionManager;
   7: import org.springframework.transaction.TransactionDefinition;
   8: import org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionTemplate;
   9: 
  10: import java.math.BigDecimal;
  11: import java.time.Instant;
  12: 
  13: /** FIX-124: commit market input before locking a position. Execute protection
  14:  * before publishing the closed-candle event, preserving that ordering. No outer
  15:  * transaction may retain candle locks while a protection transaction runs.
  16:  */
  17: @Service
  18: public class KlineTransactionCoordinator {
  19:     private static final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(KlineTransactionCoordinator.class);
  20:     private static final int MAX_ATTEMPTS = 2;
  21:     private KlineTiming timing = KlineTiming.loggingOnly();
  22:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired
  23:     public void setTiming(KlineTiming timing) { this.timing = timing; }
  24:     private final TransactionTemplate transaction;
  25:     private final Fix124ProtectionStore diagnostics;
  26: 
  27:     public KlineTransactionCoordinator(PlatformTransactionManager manager, Fix124ProtectionStore diagnostics) {
  28:         transaction = new TransactionTemplate(manager);
  29:         transaction.setPropagationBehavior(TransactionDefinition.PROPAGATION_REQUIRES_NEW);
  30:         // FIX-153: current committed ordering after consistency locks; bound JDBC transaction work.
  31:         transaction.setIsolationLevel(TransactionDefinition.ISOLATION_READ_COMMITTED);
  32:         transaction.setTimeout(30);
  33:         this.diagnostics = diagnostics;
  34:     }
  35: 
  36:     public void process(String symbol, String interval, Instant candleOpenTime, Instant observedAt,
  37:                         BigDecimal price, Runnable persistInput, Runnable protect,
  38:                         Runnable observe, Runnable publishClosed) {
  39:         var context = timing.context(symbol, interval, candleOpenTime, observedAt, null);
  40:         // Candle and exact canonical price observation commit together. A failed
  41:         // input write must not produce an unrecorded protection decision.
  42:         try {
  43:             timing.measure(context,"INPUT_TRANSACTION",false,()->transaction.executeWithoutResult(tx->persistInput.run()));
  44:             com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.Event.INPUT_COMMITTED);
  45:         }catch(RuntimeException failure){
  46:             com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.Event.INPUT_FAILED_OR_UNCONFIRMED);throw failure;
  47:         }
  48:         // FIX-131: executeWithoutResult returned successfully: this is input COMMIT, not callback completion.
  49:         try { com.crypto.client.binance.websocket.KlineReceipt.inputCommitted(symbol, interval, candleOpenTime, observedAt); }
  50:         catch (RuntimeException diagnosticFailure) { log.warn("[FIX-131][RECEIPT_DIAGNOSTIC_FAILED] symbol={}", symbol, diagnosticFailure); }
  51:         if (protect != null) {
  52:             int attempts = 0;
  53:             RuntimeException failure = null;
  54:             do {
  55:                 attempts++;
  56:                 try {
  57:                     timing.measure(context, "PROTECTION_TRANSACTION", false, () -> transaction.executeWithoutResult(tx -> protect.run()));
  58:                     failure = null;
  59:                     break;
  60:                 } catch (RuntimeException ex) {
  61:                     failure = ex;
  62:                     // FIX-153: completion returned/failed; a commit exception alone does not prove rollback.
  63:                     // Never retry a late wallet/commit error or an unknown outcome.
  64:                     if (!(ex instanceof InitialPositionLockDeadlock) || attempts == MAX_ATTEMPTS) break;
  65:                     log.warn("[FIX-124][PRODUCTION][INITIAL_LOCK_RETRY] symbol={}, candleOpenTime={}, observedAt={}, attempt={}",
  66:                             symbol, candleOpenTime, observedAt, attempts);
  67:                 }
  68:             } while (attempts < MAX_ATTEMPTS);
  69:             if (failure != null || attempts > 1) {
  70:                 String outcome = failure == null ? "RECOVERED" : "FAILED";
  71:                 if (failure == null) {
  72:                     log.info("[FIX-124][PRODUCTION][RECOVERED] symbol={}, observedAt={}, attempts={}", symbol, observedAt, attempts);
  73:                 } else {
  74:                     com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.count(com.crypto.local.LocalRuntimeDiagnostics.Event.PROTECTION_FAILED_OR_UNCONFIRMED);
  75:                     log.error("[FIX-124][PRODUCTION][FAILED] symbol={}, observedAt={}, attempts={}; input committed; protection completion failed or unconfirmed, consult durable price evidence",
  76:                             symbol, observedAt, attempts, failure);
  77:                 }
  78:                 diagnostics.record(symbol, interval, candleOpenTime, observedAt, price, attempts, outcome, failure);
  79:             }
  80:         }
  81:         if (observe != null) {
  82:             try {
  83:                 timing.measure(context, "OBSERVER_TRANSACTION", false, () -> transaction.executeWithoutResult(tx -> observe.run()));
  84:             } catch (RuntimeException ex) {
  85:                 log.warn("[FIX-124][OBSERVER_FAILED] symbol={}; input committed; protection outcome remains in durable price evidence", symbol, ex);
  86:             }
  87:         }
  88:         // AFTER_COMMIT listener still receives an actual committed transaction;
  89:         // publishing outside a transaction would silently drop the analysis event.
  90:         if (publishClosed != null) timing.measure(context, "CLOSE_DISPATCH_TRANSACTION", false,
  91:                 () -> transaction.executeWithoutResult(tx -> publishClosed.run()));
  92:     }
  93: }

  27:         c.setPassword(env.getRequiredProperty("shared-market.password"));

  56:     }

   3: import jakarta.annotation.PreDestroy;
   4: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
   5: import org.springframework.core.env.Environment;
   6: import java.nio.channels.*;
   7: import java.nio.file.*;
   8: 
   9: /** FIX-153: single-host process exclusion, never a DB lease or trading row fence.
  10:  * All starts against this DB must use the same absolute path, same host. Multi-host
  11:  * operation remains unsupported and must be prevented by deployment orchestration. */
  12: @Component("localTraderInstance")
  13: public class LocalTraderInstance {
  14:     // Keep channels strongly reachable through context shutdown: old callbacks must not
  15:     // overlap a new process just because Spring has begun destruction. OS exit releases locks.
  16:     private static final java.util.Set<FileChannel> PROCESS_CHANNELS=java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap.newKeySet();
  17:     private FileChannel channel;private FileLock lock;
  18:     public LocalTraderInstance(Environment env)throws java.io.IOException {
  19:         if(!"OFF".equals(env.getProperty("shared-market.mode","OFF")))return;
  20:         String configured=env.getProperty("local-candle.instance-lock-path");
  21:         if(configured==null || configured.isBlank())
  22:             throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-153 local-candle.instance-lock-path must be explicitly configured as an absolute path");
  23:         Path requested=Path.of(configured);
  24:         if(!requested.isAbsolute())
  25:             throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-153 local-candle.instance-lock-path must be absolute: "+configured);
  26:         Path file=requested.normalize();
  27:         Files.createDirectories(file.getParent());channel=FileChannel.open(file,StandardOpenOption.CREATE,StandardOpenOption.WRITE);
  28:         try {lock=channel.tryLock();if(lock==null)throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-153 another trader holds the configured instance lock: "+file);}
  29:         catch(RuntimeException failure){channel.close();throw failure;}
  30:         PROCESS_CHANNELS.add(channel);
  31:         org.slf4j.LoggerFactory.getLogger(getClass()).info("[FIX-153][LOCAL_INSTANCE_ACQUIRED] lockPath={}, pid={}, mode=OFF; same-host same-path exclusion, not distributed fencing",file,ProcessHandle.current().pid());
  32:     }
  33:     @PreDestroy public void shuttingDown(){
  34:         if(lock!=null)org.slf4j.LoggerFactory.getLogger(getClass()).info("[FIX-153][INSTANCE_SHUTDOWN] pid={}; instance lock retained until JVM exit, wait for process exit before next launch",ProcessHandle.current().pid());
  35:     }
  36:     /** Explicit fixture cleanup only; normal Spring destruction retains the OS lock. */
  37:     public void close()throws java.io.IOException {if(lock!=null)lock.release();if(channel!=null){channel.close();PROCESS_CHANNELS.remove(channel);}}
  38: }

  32:     @Override public void migrate(Context context) throws Exception {
  33:         Connection connection = context.getConnection();
  34:         if (!"MySQL".equals(connection.getMetaData().getDatabaseProductName())
  35:                 || connection.getMetaData().getDatabaseMajorVersion() < 8) {

  40:                 throw new SQLException("FIX-149 Flyway history must run in crypto_ai; index target is explicitly crypto_ai_v2");
  41:             }
  42:         }
  43:         try (PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement(
  44:                 "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM information_schema.tables WHERE table_schema=? AND table_name=? AND engine='InnoDB'")) {
  45:             statement.setString(1, SOURCE_SCHEMA);
  46:             statement.setString(2, TABLE);
  47:             try (ResultSet result = statement.executeQuery()) {
  48:                 if (!result.next() || result.getInt(1) != 1) {
  49:                     throw new SQLException("FIX-149 requires existing crypto_ai_v2.market_data_stream_event on the trader MySQL server InnoDB table; no empty replacement is created");
  50:                 }
  51:             }
  52:         }
  53:         List<String> additions = new ArrayList<>();
  54:         if (!existingIndexMatches(connection, OBSERVED, OBSERVED_PARTS)) {
  55:             additions.add("ADD INDEX " + OBSERVED + " (symbol,interval_code,source,classification,observed_at,symbol_sequence)");
  56:         }
  57:         if (!existingIndexMatches(connection, CLOSED, CLOSED_PARTS)) {
  58:             additions.add("ADD INDEX " + CLOSED + " (symbol,interval_code,closed,source,classification,candle_close_time DESC,symbol_sequence ASC)");
  59:         }
  60:         if (additions.isEmpty()) {
  61:             LOG.log(System.Logger.Level.INFO, "[FIX-149][INDEXES_ALREADY_PRESENT] schema=crypto_ai_v2 historySchema=crypto_ai utc=" + Instant.now());
  62:             return;
  63:         }
  64:         long previousLockWait;
  65:         try (Statement statement = connection.createStatement(); ResultSet result = statement.executeQuery("SELECT @@SESSION.lock_wait_timeout")) {
  66:             if (!result.next()) throw new SQLException("FIX-149 cannot read metadata-lock timeout");
  67:             previousLockWait = result.getLong(1);
  68:         }
  69:         Instant start = Instant.now();
  70:         long startNanos = System.nanoTime();
  71:         LOG.log(System.Logger.Level.INFO, "[FIX-149][INDEX_BUILD_START] schema=crypto_ai_v2 historySchema=crypto_ai table=" + TABLE + " startUtc=" + start);
  72:         // One ALTER builds both missing indexes. Ten seconds bounds metadata-lock wait,
  73:         // not the build duration, resource use, or every possible InnoDB wait.
  74:         try (Statement statement = connection.createStatement()) {
  75:             statement.execute("SET SESSION lock_wait_timeout=10");
  76:             try {
  77:                 statement.execute("ALTER TABLE `" + SOURCE_SCHEMA + "`.`" + TABLE + "`" + " " + String.join(", ", additions) + ", ALGORITHM=INPLACE, LOCK=NONE");
  78:                 LOG.log(System.Logger.Level.INFO, "[FIX-149][INDEX_BUILD_END] startUtc=" + start + " endUtc=" + Instant.now()
  79:                     + " durationMs=" + ((System.nanoTime() - startNanos) / 1_000_000) + " outcome=SUCCESS");
  80:             } catch (SQLException failure) {
  81:                 LOG.log(System.Logger.Level.ERROR, "[FIX-149][INDEX_BUILD_END] startUtc=" + start + " endUtc=" + Instant.now()
  82:                     + " durationMs=" + ((System.nanoTime() - startNanos) / 1_000_000) + " outcome=FAILED sqlState=" + failure.getSQLState());
  83:                 throw failure;
  84:             } finally {
  85:                 statement.execute("SET SESSION lock_wait_timeout=" + previousLockWait);
  86:             }
  87:         }
  88:     }

  82:     private List<Map<String,Object>> categoryCounts(String symbol,Instant now) {
  83:         String grace="CASE d.interval_code";

  87:         String protection="d.interval_code='1m' AND d.observed_at BETWEEN ? AND ?";
  88:         String marker="s.active_delivery_event_id=d.source_event_id AND s.active_delivery_state IN ('ACTIVE','REVIEW_REQUIRED')";
  89:         String sql="SELECT d.symbol,d.interval_code,"
  90:             +"SUM(CASE WHEN "+protection+" AND d.protection_completed_at IS NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) eligible_protection,"

  94:             +"SUM(CASE WHEN "+marker+" AND s.active_delivery_state='ACTIVE' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) owned,"
  95:             +"SUM(CASE WHEN "+marker+" AND s.active_delivery_state='REVIEW_REQUIRED' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) fenced,"

 103:     private long cadence(String interval){return switch(interval){case "1m"->60;case "5m"->300;case "15m"->900;case "1h"->3600;case "4h"->14400;case "1d"->86400;default->throw new IllegalArgumentException("Unknown interval");};}

DOC EXCERPTS
   1: # Crypto AI Trader ƒ?" Architecture
   2: 
   3: Status labels used in this document:
   4: 
   5: - **Current** ƒ?" present in the repository today.
   6: - **Pending** ƒ?" approved design direction, not implemented or deployed.
   7: - **Legacy/shared** ƒ?" existing shared-market path retained as historical evidence
   8:   while the trader moves to local-candle ownership.
   9: 
  10: ## Current release status
  11: 
  12: **FIX-153 is implemented in source but is NOT APPROVED FOR PRODUCTION.**
  13: It moves the trader toward collector-free, trader-owned local candle ingestion
  14: in `shared-market.mode=OFF`. Java 21, disposable-MySQL, crash/race, Windows
  15: two-process, soak, OFF-mode and Replay-parity proof gates remain open.
  16: 
  17: FIX-154 is a separate pending higher-timeframe design. It is not implemented
  18: and is not part of FIX-153 deployment scope.
  19: 
  20: ## System purpose
  21: 
  22: The application is a Spring Boot cryptocurrency trader that analyses Binance
  23: market data, persists candles and technical indicators, creates trade signals,
  24: and performs paper-wallet execution and position protection. Production and
  25: Replay are intended to use equivalent persisted market evidence.
  26: 
  27: ## Runtime and data ownership
  28: 
  29: | Area | Current ownership | Target direction |
  30: |---|---|---|
  31: | Trader database | `crypto_ai` | Remains the trader's only runtime database. |
  32: | Collector database | `crypto_ai_v2` | Collector remains separate and is not read/written by the trader at runtime. |
  33: | Candle ingestion | In OFF mode, trader-owned Binance ingestion writes local `crypto_ai.candle`. | Must be proven in a collector-free deployment rehearsal. |
  34: | Candle processing | Bounded lanes, close-transition tokens and a local detector are implemented. | MySQL/race/crash proof remains required. |
  35: | Trading | Paper wallet and managed positions. | Live-exchange orders are outside the local-candle automatic-resolution design. |
  36: 
  37: ## Current primary flow
  38: 
  39: ```text
  40: Binance WebSocket kline
  41:   -> BinanceKlineService
  42:   -> KlineTransactionCoordinator
  43:       -> input transaction: candle + canonical 1m market price event
  44:       -> protection transaction: LivePositionProtectionService
  45:       -> observer transaction
  46:       -> close-dispatch transaction
  47:   -> CandleClosedEvent (AFTER_COMMIT)
  48:   -> CandleAnalysisDispatcher
  49:   -> CandleClosedAnalysisWorker
  50:   -> TechnicalIndicatorService
  51:   -> AnalysisService / TradeSignal
  52:   -> SignalProcessingCoordinator
  53:   -> PaperTradingService / wallet and managed-position state
  54: ```
  55: 
  56: `KlineTransactionCoordinator` preserves input -> protection -> observer ->
  57: close-dispatch ordering. Closed-candle analysis is asynchronous and must not
  58: run on the Binance ingestion thread.
  59: 
  60: ## Current core persistence
  61: 
  62: | Table/domain | Purpose |
  63: |---|---|
  64: | `candle` | Local OHLCV candle history keyed by symbol, interval, and open time. |
  65: | `technical_indicator` | Persisted indicator snapshots derived from closed candles. |
  66: | `trade_signal` | Persisted analysis decisions and their context. |
  67: | `signal_processing_work` | Durable processing registration/status for a persisted signal. |
  68: | `market_price_event` | Canonical 1m price evidence used by protection and Replay. |
  69: | Paper wallet / position tables | Paper executions, open-position state, TP/SL/profit protection. |
  70: 
  71: ## Legacy/shared-market path
  72: 
  73: **Current but pending retirement from the trader runtime.**
  74: 
  75: The shared path includes `SharedMarketSource`, `SharedMarketConsumer`,
  76: `SharedAnalysisDispatcher`, cutover/readiness logic, and local historical
  77: tables such as `shared_market_event_delivery`, `shared_market_consumer_state`,
  78: and `shared_market_health`. These records remain evidence and must not be
  79: dropped, reset, or used to manufacture an approval.
  80: 
  81: The collector event/cursor source is `crypto_ai_v2.market_data_stream_event`
  82: and `market_data_stream_cursor`. In the target local-candle runtime, the
  83: trader must not connect to, read, or write these collector tables.
  84: 
  85: `V103__fix_149_fresh_admission_indexes` is an existing Java Flyway migration
  86: that requires the collector event table for a fresh installation. A trader-only
  87: bootstrap policy is therefore **pending** and remains a release blocker. Do
  88: not edit applied Flyway migrations or Flyway history.
  89: 
  90: ## Implemented, unverified: local-candle architecture (FIX-153)
  91: 
  92: The implemented intended mode is `shared-market.mode=OFF` with trader-owned Binance kline
  93: ingestion and local candle writes. The shared executors and direct-ingestion
  94: path must never run together.
  95: 
  96: Implemented safeguards:
  97: 
  98: - Candle writes are monotonic: a late open update cannot reopen or overwrite a
  99:   closed candle.
 100: - A closed-candle transition is admitted exactly once using an atomic upsert
 101:   result plus in-process admission coordination.
 102: - A bounded local detector recovers a closed candle that was committed before
 103:   the in-memory event/dispatcher could start analysis.
 104: - Fresh candles may execute; stale candles are analysed historically only, or
 105:   their already-owned work is expired without business execution.
 106: - Analysis lanes are bounded. Under overload, 1m may retain only the latest
 107:   pending candle; higher intervals use an explicit bounded FIFO policy.
 108: - Historic shared `REVIEW_REQUIRED` evidence must not block OFF-mode work.
 109: - OFF-mode health/admin endpoints must use local-candle health and must not
 110:   query retired shared-market tables.
 111: - Current REST backfill can finalise an interrupted open row but cannot mint a
 112:   runtime close token or dispatch analysis. Runtime close tokens plus
 113:   provenance keep backfill out of detector admission. The approved, pending
 114:   automatic-reconciliation extension will also insert an exact wholly absent
 115:   candle only inside the active quality window, with `BACKFILL` provenance and
 116:   the same no-token/no-dispatch boundary.
 117: - Observed local behavior (2026-10-09): a WebSocket reconnect at a candle-close
 118:   boundary can leave the already-persisted 1m/5m rows open because Binance does
 119:   not replay the final closed-kline event. The current detector recovers missing
 120:   analysis admission, not missing close facts. An authoritative, bounded REST
 121:   repair may therefore finalise the exact `closed=0` row or, under the approved
 122:   gap-backfill scope, insert an exact absent identity. It must preserve existing
 123:   provenance, use `BACKFILL` for an insertion, create no close-transition token,
 124:   and never dispatch historical work. Automatic reconciliation of this reconnect
 125:   case remains a FIX-153 release gate; the one-time local repair is evidence,
 126:   not proof that the gap is closed.
 127: - Approved pending reconciliation design: gap backfill is insert-only (no
 128:   duplicate-key update); it can never alter a live row that appears during a
 129:   REST request. Candidate selection is bounded to the active quality window;
 130:   terminal audit records are not retried. Every candle/work route must produce
 131:   a durable terminal outcome or remain explicitly retryable after a transient
 132:   failure. Historical-only processing has no execution authority.
 133: - The pending universal mutation barrier is an in-memory drain participant,
 134:   not a database or persistent trading fence. Outside drain it must not delay,
 135:   queue, or block fresh trading, signals, or protection. Open-position
 136:   protection during application replacement remains an unresolved release gate.
 137: - Local source now contains the first reconciliation implementation slice:
 138:   V107 durable audit/outcome tables, update-only authoritative open-row repair,
 139:   pure insert-only gap backfill, a dedicated recovery executor, a reconnect
 140:   request hook, and drain participation. It is disabled by default and remains
 141:   unvalidated on MySQL; it has no deployment approval.
 142: - Direct prices are normalised to `DECIMAL(30,12)` before persistence,
 143:   protection and completion. Equal timestamps have one canonical receipt.
 144: - Every processing attempt is freshness-checked after normal locks using the
 145:   database-to-exchange correction. JVM-origin timestamp decisions use the
 146:   separately measured JVM-to-exchange correction. The measurement performs no
 147:   HTTP under execution locks and uses a bounded five-second time-only client.
 148:   General Binance REST calls use the configured request timeout, and scheduled
 149:   clock sampling has a four-thread Spring scheduler pool.
 150:   Bootstrap obtains time/klines before opening its local candle-write
 151:   transaction, so a stalled REST request does not retain database resources.
 152:   REST bootstrap gets Binance server time before klines.
 153: - An ADMIN-only drain stops analysis admission, exits the JVM on successful
 154:   quiesce, and resumes admission on timeout. Price protection pauses during
 155:   the controlled drain window.
 156: 
 157: ## Implemented, unverified: protection price-event state machine
 158: 
 159: For direct 1m price observations, the target evidence lifecycle is:

 107:     private void notice(BinanceWebSocketHandler handler) {
 108:         if (handler.gate.state() == GenerationGate.State.REVOKED && !handler.allClosed()) {
 109:             if (current == null || current.handler != handler) cleanup.add(handler);
 110:             // After bounded shutdown, even the unresolved CURRENT handshake retains a cleanup path.
 111:             // This fallback runs only during shutdown, never on a live processing callback.
 112:             if (closeWorker.isShutdown() && handler.gate.isDrained()) handler.close();
 113:         }
 114:         wake();
 115:     }
 116:     CompletableFuture<Void> requestReload() {
 117:         if (!properties.getWebsocket().isEnabled()) throw failure(WebSocketReconnectException.Reason.CONNECTION_FAILED);
 118:         var request = new CompletableFuture<Void>();
 119:         synchronized (lifecycle) {
 120:             if (stopRequested) throw failure(WebSocketReconnectException.Reason.STOPPED);
 121:             if (!requests.offer(request)) throw failure(WebSocketReconnectException.Reason.BUSY);
 122:         }
 123:         start(); wake();
 124:         return request;
 125:     }
 126:     public void reload() {
 127:         if (sharedMarketEnabled) { return; }
 128:         var request = requestReload();
 129:         try { request.get(30, TimeUnit.SECONDS); }
 130:         catch (TimeoutException ex) { throw failure(WebSocketReconnectException.Reason.WAIT_TIMEOUT); }
 131:         catch (InterruptedException ex) { Thread.currentThread().interrupt(); throw failure(WebSocketReconnectException.Reason.WAIT_TIMEOUT); }
 132:         catch (ExecutionException ex) {
 133:             if (ex.getCause() instanceof RuntimeException runtime) throw runtime;
 134:             throw failure(WebSocketReconnectException.Reason.CONNECTION_FAILED);
 135:         }

## FINDINGS

1. The repository implements two mutually exclusive intended paths:
   - `OFF`: trader-owned Binance WebSocket ingestion, local `crypto_ai.candle` writes, local close detection/dispatch.
   - `LIVE`: collector-backed shared-market consumption from `crypto_ai_v2`.
   
   This broadly matches the pending local-candle direction in `AGENTS.md`, but it is not deployment-ready.

2. In `OFF`, the trader owns Binance kline WebSocket ingestion. The WebSocket handler calls `BinanceKlineService.processKline`, which persists the local candle and canonical 1m price event, runs protection and observer work, then publishes a close event only after an atomic close transition.

3. In `OFF`, REST bootstrap/backfill is trader-owned:
   - `MarketDataBootstrapService` invokes `MarketDataService.importCandles`.
   - It writes closed REST data to local `candle` and explicitly suppresses analysis/execution for bootstrap in OFF mode.
   - A separate reconciliation implementation exists for authoritative recovery/gap backfill, but is disabled by default.

4. Local candle persistence is implemented through `CandleRepository` writing `crypto_ai.candle` when not in LIVE mode. Runtime updates preserve monotonic closure; recovery has distinct update-only and insert-if-absent paths.

5. In `LIVE`, the trader has an explicit runtime dependency on the external collector:
   - a read-only pool connects to configured `crypto_ai_v2`;
   - `SharedMarketConsumer` reads `market_data_stream_event` / cursor tables;
   - it writes local shared-delivery/state tables and dispatches work from them.
   
   This is retained legacy/shared functionality, not the target OFF architecture.

6. Material architectural defect: `OBSERVE` is neither isolated nor mutually exclusive. `SharedMarketSource` treats only `LIVE` as enabled, so direct Binance WebSocket ingestion remains enabled in `OBSERVE`; but `SharedMarketConsumer` also starts its collector polling for every non-OFF mode. Thus `OBSERVE` can run direct ingestion and collector consumption together, violating the documented “do not run direct ingestion and shared-market executors together” rule.

7. Close handling in OFF is substantially aligned with the pending safety direction:
   - runtime upsert uses an immutable close-transition token;
   - duplicate close and late-open updates do not re-admit work;
   - the transaction order is input → protection → observer → close dispatch;
   - analysis is dispatched asynchronously, not on the WebSocket callback thread.

8. Recovery/backfill behavior is partly implemented but operationally inactive by default. The reconciler requires `OFF` and `local-candle.reconciliation.enabled=true`; the checked-in default is `false`. Recovery finalizes existing open rows without a token; gap recovery is insert-if-absent with `BACKFILL` provenance and records a non-live terminal outcome. It does not dispatch analysis or execution.

9. Replay uses candle data through `CandleRepository`: local `crypto_ai.candle` in OFF and the collector-side reader in LIVE. Replay also consumes persisted `market_price_event` evidence for exact-price behavior when available. I found source-level parity machinery, but no executed Replay validation evidence in this investigation.

10. The current default configuration is OFF, but the checked-in default cannot start OFF mode as-is: `local-candle.instance-lock-path` is empty while startup requires an explicit absolute path. This is a deliberate fail-fast control, but it means default OFF configuration is not independently runnable.

11. A fresh trader-only install remains blocked by applied Java Flyway migration `V103`: it requires `crypto_ai_v2.market_data_stream_event`. This directly contradicts a fully collector-free bootstrap, though both AGENTS.md and the architecture documents acknowledge it as an unresolved release blocker.

12. Documentation is generally candid that FIX-153 is source-implemented but unverified/not approved for production. However, its “current primary flow” describes OFF mode as the current architecture without equally prominently describing the actual OBSERVE dual-path contradiction and the disabled reconciliation default.

## EVIDENCE

- Direct WebSocket ownership:
  - [BinanceWebSocketHandler.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/client/binance/websocket/BinanceWebSocketHandler.java:58) constructs receipt evidence and calls `BinanceKlineService.processKline`.
  - [BinanceWebSocketManager.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/client/binance/websocket/BinanceWebSocketManager.java:95) starts the trader WebSocket unless `sharedMarketEnabled` is true.
  - [BinanceKlineService.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/BinanceKlineService.java:101) writes runtime candle data and price evidence; [line 110](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/BinanceKlineService.java:110) publishes only a confirmed close transition.

- Transaction order and asynchronous dispatch:
  - [KlineTransactionCoordinator.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/infrastructure/transaction/KlineTransactionCoordinator.java:40) commits input first; [line 51](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/infrastructure/transaction/KlineTransactionCoordinator.java:51) runs protection; [line 81](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/infrastructure/transaction/KlineTransactionCoordinator.java:81) observer; [line 90](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/infrastructure/transaction/KlineTransactionCoordinator.java:90) close dispatch.
  - [CandleAnalysisDispatcher.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/indicator/event/CandleAnalysisDispatcher.java:106) executes work on its executor, not the ingestion callback.

- Local persistence and monotonic close:
  - [CandleRepository.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/repository/CandleRepository.java:109) defines runtime upsert; [line 120](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/repository/CandleRepository.java:120) recognizes closure only by matching token.
  - [CandleRepository.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/repository/CandleRepository.java:132) performs recovery as update-only for existing open rows.
  - [CandleRepository.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/repository/CandleRepository.java:152) uses insert-if-absent for absent recovery identities.
  - [LocalClosedCandleDetector.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/local/LocalClosedCandleDetector.java:50) admits only closed, tokenized, non-BACKFILL candles.

- REST/bootstrap/recovery:
  - [MarketDataService.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/MarketDataService.java:31) fetches Binance REST klines and writes them locally.
  - [MarketDataBootstrapService.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/MarketDataBootstrapService.java:105) makes OFF-mode bootstrap data-only: no analysis/execution.
  - [LocalCandleReconciliationService.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/local/LocalCandleReconciliationService.java:92) runs only when enabled and OFF; [line 183](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/local/LocalCandleReconciliationService.java:183) differentiates recovery from gap backfill; [line 189](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/local/LocalCandleReconciliationService.java:189) records `SKIPPED_NOT_LIVE`.

- Configuration:
  - [application.yml](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/resources/application.yml:40) sets reconciliation disabled.
  - [application.yml](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/resources/application.yml:417) sets default `shared-market.mode=OFF`.
  - [application.yml](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/resources/application.yml:454) leaves the required instance-lock path empty.
  - [LocalTraderInstance.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/local/LocalTraderInstance.java:20) fails OFF startup if that path is absent or non-absolute.

- Collector/shared coupling:
  - [SharedMarketSource.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/shared/SharedMarketSource.java:20) supports `OFF`, `OBSERVE`, and `LIVE`; [line 22](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/shared/SharedMarketSource.java:22) enables shared mode only for `LIVE`; [lines 25–36](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/shared/SharedMarketSource.java:25) create the collector read pool for non-OFF.
  - [SharedMarketConsumer.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/shared/SharedMarketConsumer.java:136) returns only for OFF and schedules collector discovery/price/analysis consumers for non-OFF at [lines 146–149](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/shared/SharedMarketConsumer.java:146).
  - [SharedMarketConsumer.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/shared/SharedMarketConsumer.java:345) reads `market_data_stream_event`.

- OBSERVE contradiction:
  - `OBSERVE` makes `sharedMarketEnabled=false` by [SharedMarketSource.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/shared/SharedMarketSource.java:22).
  - The WebSocket therefore starts because it exits only when that boolean is true: [BinanceWebSocketManager.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/client/binance/websocket/BinanceWebSocketManager.java:95).
  - The shared consumer also starts because it exits only for OFF: [SharedMarketConsumer.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/shared/SharedMarketConsumer.java:136).

- Replay:
  - [RegressionTestWorker.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/RegressionTestWorker.java:129) loads candles through the same repository abstraction.
  - [ShadowProductionReplayService.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/ShadowProductionReplayService.java:85) selects persisted live-price evidence where present and labels fallback behavior separately.

- Bootstrap blocker:
  - [V103__fix_149_fresh_admission_indexes.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/db/migration/V103__fix_149_fresh_admission_indexes.java:43) checks for `crypto_ai_v2.market_data_stream_event`; [line 49](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/db/migration/V103__fix_149_fresh_admission_indexes.java:49) fails if absent.
  - [AGENTS.md](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/AGENTS.md:57) identifies this as a trader-only bootstrap release blocker.
  - [codex-docs/ARCHITECTURE.md](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/codex-docs/ARCHITECTURE.md:85) reports the same blocker.

## OBJECTIONS

- `OBSERVE` permits concurrent direct Binance ingestion and shared collector execution. This materially violates the pending direction’s exclusivity requirement and makes the mode unsafe as an architectural transition/observation mode.
- Reconciliation is disabled by default, so the source implementation does not establish operational recovery coverage for missed close events.
- A default OFF launch fails without external lock-path configuration; this is safe fail-fast behavior, but means the checked-in default is not a runnable local-candle deployment configuration.
- Fresh trader-only installation still requires the collector schema due to V103; the trader is not bootstrap-independent.
- No source-only inspection can establish production configuration, migration state, deployed artifact identity, reconnect behavior, crash/restart properties, MySQL concurrency behavior, or Replay parity execution.

Candidate for later solution review only: make the mode model explicitly exclusive—either disable direct ingestion in OBSERVE or prohibit OBSERVE while the direct path is enabled—and verify this with an integration test that asserts only one ingestion architecture starts.

## UNKNOWNS

- Whether any deployed environment is using OFF, LIVE, or OBSERVE.
- Whether reconciliation has been enabled in an approved runtime configuration.
- Whether production satisfies the required absolute instance lock and local schema prerequisites.
- Whether the required MySQL/Java 21/race/crash/Replay parity gates have passed.
- Whether collector-free bootstrap has a tested forward migration/installer policy.
- Whether current source changes are the exact artifact used by any runtime; no git, database, runtime, or production system was accessed.
